The code reads the figure stage's one series_axis per panel (ingest/final/<pid>/figures.json), so it takes no wrong axis: no change. ela03 fig5a, 9a, 10a, 11a: series axis 'Exposure Times (s)', legends bare numbers ('19', '46', '65' on fig11a: no words, no 'd'); yim22 fig5a / 5c 'H (µm)', fig5b / 5d 'N (-)', fig4e 'q'. 46 of the 47 are other because the legend path resolves the label whole with its unit group, while the figure import resolves its key without it (Exposure Times -> pulse_time, H -> feature_width, N -> cycle_number); fig4e 'q' resolves in neither. Listed for a decision (P190.2_for_decision): resolving the key as the import does would write fig11a's 19 / 46 / 65 as pulse_time (s), against pass 1's diameters.
